# Photo credits

All photos are from Wikimedia Commons (many located via the WorldCuisines food-kb dataset's image links, the rest via Wikipedia article images or Commons search), resized to ~800px wide and recompressed as JPEG for offline use. Each remains under its original license (linked). Changes: resized/cropped/recompressed.
